// QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version
// 
// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters
// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point
// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

static const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;
static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;

// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
struct Point {
    double x, y;
};

// Structure to represent a cluster
struct Cluster {
    std::vector<int> members;
    int seed_point;
};

// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
    
    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
    int count = 0;
    
    while (count < N) {
        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
        
        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
            group_cnt = N - count;
        }
        
        while (group_cnt > 0) {
            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
            const double r = frand() * R;
            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
            
            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
                continue;
            }
            
            points[count] = {x, y};
            count++;
            group_cnt--;
        }
    }
}

// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
}

// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
// Returns -1 if no such point exists
int findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,
                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold,
                     const int point_count) {
    int closest_point = -1;
    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
    
    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate
    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster
        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
        
        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members
        double max_dist = 0.0;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = cluster_members[i];
            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);
        }
        
        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
            min_diameter = max_dist;
            closest_point = candidate;
        }
    }
    
    return closest_point;
}

// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
                              const std::vector<Point>& points,
                              const double threshold,
                              const int point_count,
                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {
    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);
    std::vector<int> members;
    
    // Add seed point
    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;
    members.push_back(seed_point);
    
    // Iteratively add closest points
    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {
        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, 
                                             threshold, point_count);
        
        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added
        
        in_cluster[closest] = true;
        members.push_back(closest);
    }
    
    // Copy members if requested
    if (cluster_members) {
        *cluster_members = members;
    }
    
    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
}

// Main QT clustering algorithm
std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                  const double threshold) {
    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
    
    // Initialize unclustered indices
    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
    }
    
    // Main clustering loop
    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
        int max_cardinality = -1;
        int best_seed = -1;
        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
        
        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
            
            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
                                                       threshold, N, 
                                                       &candidate_members);
            
            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
                max_cardinality = cardinality;
                best_seed = seed;
                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
            }
        }
        
        // If we found a cluster, add it
        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
            Cluster cluster;
            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
            clusters.push_back(cluster);
            
            // Mark all members as clustered
            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
            }
            
            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
            unclustered_indices.erase(
                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
                unclustered_indices.end()
            );
        } else {
            // No more clusters can be formed
            break;
        }
    }
    
    return clusters;
}

// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties
bool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold) {
    bool valid = true;
    
    printf("Validating clusters:\n");
    
    // Check each cluster
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];
        double max_diameter = 0.0;
        
        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {
            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {
                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], 
                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);
                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);
            }
        }
        
        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters
            printf("  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\n", 
                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);
        }
        
        // Validate diameter is within threshold
        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error
            printf("ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\n", 
                   c, max_diameter, threshold);
            valid = false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check for duplicate memberships
    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];
            if (membership[member] >= 0) {
                printf("ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\n",
                       member, membership[member], c);
                valid = false;
            }
            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);
        }
    }
    
    // Count clustered points
    int clustered_count = 0;

    int clustered_count = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {
        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;
    }
    
    printf("Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\n",
           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);
    
    return valid;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\n");
    printf("  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int num_points = 1000;
    double threshold = 2.0;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {
        printf("Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\n", 
               num_points, threshold);
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("QT Clustering Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of points: %d\n", num_points);
    printf("Distance threshold: %.2f\n", threshold);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Generate synthetic data
    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);
    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);
    
    // Perform QT clustering
    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);
    
    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
        cluster_end - cluster_start);
    
    printf("Clustering time: %ld ms\n", cluster_time.count());
    printf("Clusters found: %zu\n", clusters.size());
    
    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics
    int total_clustered = 0;
    int max_cluster_size = 0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {
        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());
        total_clustered += size;
        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);
    }
    
    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 : 
        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();
    
    printf("Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\n", 
           total_clustered, num_points, 
           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);
    printf("Average cluster size: %.2f\n", avg_cluster_size);
    printf("Maximum cluster size: %d\n", max_cluster_size);
    
    // Performance metrics
    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;
    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;
    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;
    printf("Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\n", 
           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing
        std::vector<double> membershipData;
        membershipData.reserve(num_points);
        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);
        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);
            }
        }
        for (int m : membership) {
            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));
        }
        print_results(membershipData, "ClusterMembership");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// 
// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters
// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point
// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

static const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;
static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;

// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
struct Point {
    double x, y;
};

// Structure to represent a cluster
struct Cluster {
    std::vector<int> members;
    int seed_point;
};

// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
    
    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
    int count = 0;
    
    while (count < N) {
        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
        
        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
            group_cnt = N - count;
        }
        
        while (group_cnt > 0) {
            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
            const double r = frand() * R;
            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
            
            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
                continue;
            }
            
            points[count] = {x, y};
            count++;
            group_cnt--;
        }
    }
}

// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
}

// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
// Returns -1 if no such point exists
int findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,
                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold,
                     const int point_count) {
    int closest_point = -1;
    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
    
    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate
    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster
        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
        
        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members
        double max_dist = 0.0;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = cluster_members[i];
            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);
        }
        
        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
            min_diameter = max_dist;
            closest_point = candidate;
        }
    }
    
    return closest_point;
}

// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
                              const std::vector<Point>& points,
                              const double threshold,
                              const int point_count,
                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {
    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);
    std::vector<int> members;
    
    // Add seed point
    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;
    members.push_back(seed_point);
    
    // Iteratively add closest points
    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {
        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, 
                                             threshold, point_count);
        
        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added
        
        in_cluster[closest] = true;
        members.push_back(closest);
    }
    
    // Copy members if requested
    if (cluster_members) {
        *cluster_members = members;
    }
    
    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
}

// Main QT clustering algorithm
std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                  const double threshold) {
    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
    
    // Initialize unclustered indices
    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
    }
    
    // Main clustering loop
    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
        int max_cardinality = -1;
        int best_seed = -1;
        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
        
        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
            
            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
                                                       threshold, N, 
                                                       &candidate_members);
            
            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
                max_cardinality = cardinality;
                best_seed = seed;
                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
            }
        }
        
        // If we found a cluster, add it
        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
            Cluster cluster;
            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
            clusters.push_back(cluster);
            
            // Mark all members as clustered
            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
fatal: not a git repository (or any parent up to mount point /)

codex
The main source of work is the independent candidate-cluster construction for every remaining seed. I’ll parallelize that outer search with deterministic tie-breaking, and I’m also targeting the repeated distance recomputation inside each candidate: the original recalculates every member-to-candidate distance on every growth step, which inflates the work substantially without affecting the algorithm’s decisions.

+            // Dynamic scheduling balances seeds whose clusters grow to
+            // different sizes.

+            for (int position = 0;

+

+                closest_position = position;

+        }

+        // original lowest-index tie breaking.

+    // makes the update for a newly-added member a cache-friendly row traversal

+            // different sizes.

+                    done = true;

+                    done = true;

+            // done flag before every thread evaluates the next iteration.

codex
The first OpenMP build is clean. The implementation now keeps one worker team alive for the whole clustering run, assigns seed candidates dynamically, and resolves each round’s winner deterministically. It also reuses per-thread scratch storage and a shared distance table, while preserving the original point-order tie rules. I’m now checking hashes across thresholds and thread counts before tuning scheduling and memory behavior.

+    // max_distances[p] is maintained incrementally.  After a point is added,
+    // only its distance to every remaining point is new; recomputing distances
+    // to all earlier members would produce the same maximum at much higher cost.
+    int newest_member = seed_point;
+    while (static_cast<int>(workspace.members.size()) < active_count) {
+        int closest_position = -1;
+        double min_diameter = threshold;
+        const double* distance_row =
+            distances.data() + static_cast<size_t>(newest_member) * point_count;
+
+        // unclustered_indices remains sorted, so strict comparison retains the
+        // original lowest-index tie breaking.
+        for (int position = 0; position < active_count; ++position) {
+            const int candidate = unclustered_indices[position];
+            const double candidate_diameter = std::max(
+                workspace.max_distances[position], distance_row[candidate]);
+            workspace.max_distances[position] = candidate_diameter;
+
+            if (candidate_diameter < min_diameter) {
+                min_diameter = candidate_diameter;
+                closest_position = position;
+            }
+        }
+
+        if (closest_position < 0) {
+            break;
+        }
+
+        // Infinity makes selected positions ineligible in subsequent scans
+        // without a separate membership bitmap or an unpredictable branch.
+        workspace.max_distances[closest_position] =
+            std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
+        newest_member = unclustered_indices[closest_position];
+        workspace.members.push_back(newest_member);
     }
-    
-    // Copy members if requested
-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
-    }
-    
-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+
+    return static_cast<int>(workspace.members.size());
 }

+            // Dynamic scheduling balances seeds whose clusters grow to
+            // different sizes.
+#pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)

+                 position < static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                 ++position) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[position];
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    position, unclustered_indices, distances, threshold, N,
+                    workspace);
+

+                    (cardinality == local_best.cardinality &&
+                     seed < local_best.seed_point)) {

-                unclustered_indices.end()

+                for (int thread = 0; thread < team_size; ++thread) {
+                    if (thread_bests[thread].cardinality < 0) {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    if (winner < 0 ||
+                        thread_bests[thread].cardinality >

+                        winner = thread;
+                    }
+                }
+

+                    cluster.members = thread_bests[winner].members;

+                                return selected[point] != 0;
+                            }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+                } else {
+                    done = true;
+                }
+
+                if (unclustered_indices.empty()) {
+                    done = true;
+                }
+            }
+            // The implicit single barrier publishes the updated active set and
+            // done flag before every thread evaluates the next iteration.

     }
-    
+

                                   const double threshold) {

+    // Distances are reused by many seed candidates.  A full row-major matrix
+    // makes the update for a newly-added member a cache-friendly row traversal
+    // and computes each directed distance only once.
+    const size_t matrix_size = static_cast<size_t>(N) * static_cast<size_t>(N);
+    std::vector<double> distances(matrix_size);

+    std::vector<ThreadBest> thread_bests(max_threads);

+    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all QT iterations.  This is important
+    // as clusters get smaller: repeatedly creating a team would eventually
+    // cost more than the remaining candidate work.
+#pragma omp parallel shared(done, distances, unclustered_indices, clusters, workspaces, thread_bests)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        CandidateWorkspace& workspace = workspaces[thread_id];
+        ThreadBest& local_best = thread_bests[thread_id];
+

+                distances.data() + static_cast<size_t>(member) * N;
+#pragma omp simd

+
+        while (!done) {
+            local_best.reset();
+
+            // Every seed candidate reads the same immutable active-point set.
+            // Dynamic scheduling balances seeds whose clusters grow to
+            // different sizes.
+#pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (int position = 0;
+                 position < static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                 ++position) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[position];
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    position, unclustered_indices, distances, threshold, N,
+                    workspace);
+
+                if (cardinality > local_best.cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best.cardinality &&
+                     seed < local_best.seed_point)) {

+                    local_best.seed_point = seed;
+                    local_best.members = workspace.members;
+                }
             }
-

-                unclustered_indices.end()

-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;

+            // largest cardinality, then the first (lowest-index) seed.
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                const int team_size = omp_get_num_threads();
+                int winner = -1;
+                for (int thread = 0; thread < team_size; ++thread) {
+                    if (thread_bests[thread].cardinality < 0) {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    if (winner < 0 ||
+                        thread_bests[thread].cardinality >
+                            thread_bests[winner].cardinality ||
+                        (thread_bests[thread].cardinality ==
+                             thread_bests[winner].cardinality &&
+                         thread_bests[thread].seed_point <
+                             thread_bests[winner].seed_point)) {
+                        winner = thread;
+                    }
+                }
+
+                if (winner >= 0 && thread_bests[winner].cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = thread_bests[winner].seed_point;
+                    cluster.members = thread_bests[winner].members;
+
+                    std::vector<unsigned char> selected(N, 0);
+                    for (const int member : cluster.members) {
+                        selected[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(
+                            unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                            unclustered_indices.end(),
+                            [&selected](const int point) {
+                                return selected[point] != 0;
+                            }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+                } else {
+                    done = true;
+                }
+
+                if (unclustered_indices.empty()) {
+                    done = true;
+                }
+            }
+            // The implicit single barrier publishes the updated active set and
+            // done flag before every thread evaluates the next iteration.
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

+                    }
+                }

+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = thread_bests[winner].seed_point;
+                    cluster.members = thread_bests[winner].members;

+                            unclustered_indices.end(),
+                            [&selected](const int point) {
+                                return selected[point] != 0;
+                            }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+                } else {
+                    done = true;
+                }

                                   const double threshold) {

+    // Distances are reused by many seed candidates.  A full row-major matrix
+    // makes the update for a newly-added member a cache-friendly row traversal
+    // and computes each directed distance only once.
+    const size_t matrix_size = static_cast<size_t>(N) * static_cast<size_t>(N);
+    std::vector<double> distances(matrix_size);

+    std::vector<CandidateWorkspace> workspaces(max_threads);
+    std::vector<ThreadBest> thread_bests(max_threads);
+    bool done = unclustered_indices.empty();
+
+    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all QT iterations.  This is important
+    // as clusters get smaller: repeatedly creating a team would eventually
+    // cost more than the remaining candidate work.
+#pragma omp parallel shared(done, distances, unclustered_indices, clusters, workspaces, thread_bests)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        CandidateWorkspace& workspace = workspaces[thread_id];
+        ThreadBest& local_best = thread_bests[thread_id];
+
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (int member = 0; member < N; ++member) {
+            double* distance_row =
+                distances.data() + static_cast<size_t>(member) * N;
+#pragma omp simd
+            for (int candidate = 0; candidate < N; ++candidate) {
+                // Keep the argument order identical to the sequential search.
+                distance_row[candidate] =

+

+            // Dynamic scheduling balances seeds whose clusters grow to
+            // different sizes.
+#pragma omp for schedule(runtime)
+            for (int position = 0;
+                 position < static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                 ++position) {

+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    position, unclustered_indices, distances, threshold, N,
+                    workspace);
+
+                if (cardinality > local_best.cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best.cardinality &&

+            // largest cardinality, then the first (lowest-index) seed.

+            {
+                const int team_size = omp_get_num_threads();
+                int winner = -1;
+                for (int thread = 0; thread < team_size; ++thread) {
+                    if (thread_bests[thread].cardinality < 0) {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    if (winner < 0 ||
+                        thread_bests[thread].cardinality >
+                            thread_bests[winner].cardinality ||
+                        (thread_bests[thread].cardinality ==
+                             thread_bests[winner].cardinality &&
+                         thread_bests[thread].seed_point <
+                             thread_bests[winner].seed_point)) {
+                        winner = thread;
+                    }
+                }
+
+                if (winner >= 0 && thread_bests[winner].cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = thread_bests[winner].seed_point;
+                    cluster.members = thread_bests[winner].members;
+
+                    std::vector<unsigned char> selected(N, 0);
+                    for (const int member : cluster.members) {
+                        selected[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(
+                            unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                            unclustered_indices.end(),
+                            [&selected](const int point) {
+                                return selected[point] != 0;
+                            }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+                } else {
+                    done = true;
+                }
+
+                if (unclustered_indices.empty()) {
+                    done = true;
+                }
+            }
+            // The implicit single barrier publishes the updated active set and
+            // done flag before every thread evaluates the next iteration.
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;

+    workspace.members.clear();

+    workspace.max_distances[seed_position] =
+        std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
+    workspace.members.push_back(seed_point);

+    // max_distances[p] is maintained incrementally.  After a point is added,
+    // only its distance to every remaining point is new; recomputing distances
+    // to all earlier members would produce the same maximum at much higher cost.
+    int newest_member = seed_point;

+        const double* distance_row =
+            distances.data() + static_cast<size_t>(newest_member) * point_count;

+        // original lowest-index tie breaking.
+        for (int position = 0; position < active_count; ++position) {
+            const int candidate = unclustered_indices[position];
+            const double candidate_diameter = std::max(

+            workspace.max_distances[position] = candidate_diameter;
+
+            if (candidate_diameter < min_diameter) {
+                min_diameter = candidate_diameter;
+                closest_position = position;
+            }
+        }
+
+        if (closest_position < 0) {
+            break;
+        }
+
+        // Infinity makes selected positions ineligible in subsequent scans
+        // without a separate membership bitmap or an unpredictable branch.
+        workspace.max_distances[closest_position] =
+            std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
+        newest_member = unclustered_indices[closest_position];
+        workspace.members.push_back(newest_member);

-    // Copy members if requested

-        *cluster_members = members;
-    }
-

+    return static_cast<int>(workspace.members.size());

                                   const double threshold) {

+    // makes the update for a newly-added member a cache-friendly row traversal
+    // and computes each directed distance only once.
+    const size_t matrix_size = static_cast<size_t>(N) * static_cast<size_t>(N);
+    std::vector<double> distances(matrix_size);

+    std::vector<ThreadBest> thread_bests(max_threads);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> selected(N, 0);
+    bool done = unclustered_indices.empty();

+    // as clusters get smaller: repeatedly creating a team would eventually
+    // cost more than the remaining candidate work.
+#pragma omp parallel shared(done, distances, unclustered_indices, clusters, workspaces, thread_bests, selected)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        CandidateWorkspace& workspace = workspaces[thread_id];
+        ThreadBest& local_best = thread_bests[thread_id];
+
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (int member = 0; member < N; ++member) {
+            double* distance_row =
+                distances.data() + static_cast<size_t>(member) * N;
+#pragma omp simd
+            for (int candidate = 0; candidate < N; ++candidate) {

+            for (int position = 0;

+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(

+                    workspace);

+                if (cardinality > local_best.cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best.cardinality &&
+                     seed < local_best.seed_point)) {
+                    local_best.cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best.seed_point = seed;
+                    local_best.members = workspace.members;

+                        selected[member] = 1;
+                    }

+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(

+                            unclustered_indices.end(),

+                                return selected[point] != 0;
+                            }),

+                }

 // Main QT clustering algorithm
 std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                   const double threshold) {
     const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
-    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
     std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
     std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
-    
+
     // Initialize unclustered indices
+    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);
+    clusters.reserve(N);
     for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
         unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
     }
-    
-    // Main clustering loop
-    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
-        int max_cardinality = -1;
-        int best_seed = -1;
-        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
-        
-        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
-            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
-            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
-            
-            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
-            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
-                                                       threshold, N, 
-                                                       &candidate_members);
-            
-            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
-                max_cardinality = cardinality;
-                best_seed = seed;
-                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
+
+    // Distances are reused by many seed candidates.  A full row-major matrix
+    // makes the update for a newly-added member a cache-friendly row traversal
+    // and computes each directed distance only once.
+    const size_t matrix_size = static_cast<size_t>(N) * static_cast<size_t>(N);
+    std::vector<double> distances(matrix_size);
+
+    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();
+    std::vector<CandidateWorkspace> workspaces(max_threads);
+    std::vector<ThreadBest> thread_bests(max_threads);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> selected(N, 0);
+    bool done = unclustered_indices.empty();
+
+    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all QT iterations.  This is important
+    // as clusters get smaller: repeatedly creating a team would eventually
+    // cost more than the remaining candidate work.
+#pragma omp parallel shared(done, distances, unclustered_indices, clusters, workspaces, thread_bests, selected)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        CandidateWorkspace& workspace = workspaces[thread_id];
+        ThreadBest& local_best = thread_bests[thread_id];
+
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (int member = 0; member < N; ++member) {
+            double* distance_row =
+                distances.data() + static_cast<size_t>(member) * N;
+#pragma omp simd
+            for (int candidate = 0; candidate < N; ++candidate) {
+                // Keep the argument order identical to the sequential search.
+                distance_row[candidate] =
+                    distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
             }
         }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+        while (!done) {
+            local_best.reset();
+
+            // Every seed candidate reads the same immutable active-point set.
+            // Dynamic scheduling balances seeds whose clusters grow to
+            // different sizes.
+#pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (int position = 0;
+                 position < static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                 ++position) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[position];
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    position, unclustered_indices, distances, threshold, N,
+                    workspace);
+
+                if (cardinality > local_best.cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best.cardinality &&
+                     seed < local_best.seed_point)) {
+                    local_best.cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best.seed_point = seed;
+                    local_best.members = workspace.members;
+                }
+            }
+
+            // The serial choice preserves the original deterministic rule:
+            // largest cardinality, then the first (lowest-index) seed.
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                const int team_size = omp_get_num_threads();
+                int winner = -1;
+                for (int thread = 0; thread < team_size; ++thread) {
+                    if (thread_bests[thread].cardinality < 0) {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    if (winner < 0 ||
+                        thread_bests[thread].cardinality >
+                            thread_bests[winner].cardinality ||
+                        (thread_bests[thread].cardinality ==
+                             thread_bests[winner].cardinality &&
+                         thread_bests[thread].seed_point <
+                             thread_bests[winner].seed_point)) {
+                        winner = thread;
+                    }
+                }
+
+                if (winner >= 0 && thread_bests[winner].cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = thread_bests[winner].seed_point;
+                    cluster.members = thread_bests[winner].members;
+
+                    for (const int member : cluster.members) {
+                        selected[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(
+                            unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                            unclustered_indices.end(),
+                            [&selected](const int point) {
+                                return selected[point] != 0;
+                            }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+                } else {
+                    done = true;
+                }
+
+                if (unclustered_indices.empty()) {
+                    done = true;
+                }
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+            // The implicit single barrier publishes the updated active set and
+            // done flag before every thread evaluates the next iteration.
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

+#pragma omp parallel shared(done, distances, unclustered_indices, clusters, workspaces, thread_bests, selected)

+            // different sizes.

+            for (int position = 0;
+                 position < static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                 ++position) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[position];
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    position, unclustered_indices, distances, threshold, N,
+                    workspace);
+

+                    (cardinality == local_best.cardinality &&
+                     seed < local_best.seed_point)) {

+                    local_best.seed_point = seed;
+                    local_best.members = workspace.members;

+                    }

+                        std::remove_if(
+                            unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                            unclustered_indices.end(),

     1	// QT Clustering Benchmark - OpenMP Version
     2	// 
     3	// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters
     4	// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point
     5	// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.
     6	
     7	#include <algorithm>
     8	#include <chrono>
     9	#include <cmath>
    10	#include <cstdio>
    11	#include <cstdlib>
    12	#include <cstring>
    13	#include <limits>
    14	#include <vector>
    15	
    16	#include <omp.h>
    17	
    18	#include "../common/results_output.hpp"
    19	
    20	static const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;
    21	static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;
    22	
    23	// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
    24	struct Point {
    25	    double x, y;
    26	};
    27	
    28	// Structure to represent a cluster
    29	struct Cluster {
    30	    std::vector<int> members;
    31	    int seed_point;
    32	};
    33	
    34	// Per-thread storage is retained for the whole clustering operation.  This
    35	// avoids allocating O(N) temporary arrays for every seed point.
    36	struct alignas(64) CandidateWorkspace {
    37	    std::vector<double> max_distances;
    38	    std::vector<int> members;
    39	};
    40	
    41	struct alignas(64) ThreadBest {
    42	    int cardinality = -1;
    43	    int seed_point = -1;
    44	    std::vector<int> members;
    45	
    46	    void reset() {
    47	        cardinality = -1;
    48	        seed_point = -1;
    49	        members.clear();
    50	    }
    51	};
    52	
    53	// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
    54	void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    55	    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
    56	    
    57	    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
    58	    int count = 0;
    59	    
    60	    while (count < N) {
    61	        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
    62	        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
    63	        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
    64	        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
    65	        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
    66	        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
    67	        
    68	        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
    69	        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
    70	            group_cnt = N - count;
    71	        }
    72	        
    73	        while (group_cnt > 0) {
    74	            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
    75	            const double r = frand() * R;
    76	            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
    77	            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
    78	            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
    79	            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
    80	            
    81	            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
    82	                continue;
    83	            }
    84	            
    85	            points[count] = {x, y};
    86	            count++;
    87	            group_cnt--;
    88	        }
    89	    }
    90	}
    91	
    92	// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
    93	inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    94	    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    95	    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    96	    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
    97	}
    98	
    99	// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
   100	// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
   101	int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_position,
   102	                              const std::vector<int>& unclustered_indices,
   103	                              const std::vector<double>& distances,
   104	                              const double threshold,
   105	                              const int point_count,
   106	                              CandidateWorkspace& workspace) {
   107	    const int active_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
   108	    workspace.max_distances.assign(active_count, 0.0);
   109	    workspace.members.clear();
   110	    workspace.members.reserve(active_count);
   111	
   112	    const int seed_point = unclustered_indices[seed_position];
   113	    workspace.max_distances[seed_position] =
   114	        std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
   115	    workspace.members.push_back(seed_point);
   116	
   117	    // max_distances[p] is maintained incrementally.  After a point is added,
   118	    // only its distance to every remaining point is new; recomputing distances
   119	    // to all earlier members would produce the same maximum at much higher cost.
   120	    int newest_member = seed_point;
   121	    while (static_cast<int>(workspace.members.size()) < active_count) {
   122	        int closest_position = -1;
   123	        double min_diameter = threshold;
   124	        const double* distance_row =
   125	            distances.data() + static_cast<size_t>(newest_member) * point_count;
   126	
   127	        // unclustered_indices remains sorted, so strict comparison retains the
   128	        // original lowest-index tie breaking.
   129	        for (int position = 0; position < active_count; ++position) {
   130	            const int candidate = unclustered_indices[position];
   131	            const double candidate_diameter = std::max(
   132	                workspace.max_distances[position], distance_row[candidate]);
   133	            workspace.max_distances[position] = candidate_diameter;
   134	
   135	            if (candidate_diameter < min_diameter) {
   136	                min_diameter = candidate_diameter;
   137	                closest_position = position;
   138	            }
   139	        }
   140	
   141	        if (closest_position < 0) {
   142	            break;
   143	        }
   144	
   145	        // Infinity makes selected positions ineligible in subsequent scans
   146	        // without a separate membership bitmap or an unpredictable branch.
   147	        workspace.max_distances[closest_position] =
   148	            std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
   149	        newest_member = unclustered_indices[closest_position];
   150	        workspace.members.push_back(newest_member);
   151	    }
   152	
   153	    return static_cast<int>(workspace.members.size());
   154	}
   155	
   156	// Main QT clustering algorithm
   157	std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
   158	                                  const double threshold) {
   159	    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
   160	    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
   161	    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
   162	
   163	    // Initialize unclustered indices
   164	    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);
   165	    clusters.reserve(N);
   166	    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
   167	        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
   168	    }
   169	
   170	    // Distances are reused by many seed candidates.  A full row-major matrix
   171	    // makes the update for a newly-added member a cache-friendly row traversal
   172	    // and computes each directed distance only once.
   173	    const size_t matrix_size = static_cast<size_t>(N) * static_cast<size_t>(N);
   174	    std::vector<double> distances(matrix_size);
   175	
   176	    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();
   177	    std::vector<CandidateWorkspace> workspaces(max_threads);
   178	    std::vector<ThreadBest> thread_bests(max_threads);
   179	    std::vector<unsigned char> selected(N, 0);
   180	    bool done = unclustered_indices.empty();
   181	
   182	    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all QT iterations.  This is important
   183	    // as clusters get smaller: repeatedly creating a team would eventually
   184	    // cost more than the remaining candidate work.
   185	#pragma omp parallel shared(done, distances, unclustered_indices, clusters, workspaces, thread_bests, selected)
   186	    {
   187	        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
   188	        CandidateWorkspace& workspace = workspaces[thread_id];
   189	        ThreadBest& local_best = thread_bests[thread_id];
   190	
   191	#pragma omp for schedule(static)
   192	        for (int member = 0; member < N; ++member) {
   193	            double* distance_row =
   194	                distances.data() + static_cast<size_t>(member) * N;
   195	#pragma omp simd
   196	            for (int candidate = 0; candidate < N; ++candidate) {
   197	                // Keep the argument order identical to the sequential search.
   198	                distance_row[candidate] =
   199	                    distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
   200	            }
   201	        }
   202	
   203	        while (!done) {
   204	            local_best.reset();
   205	
   206	            // Every seed candidate reads the same immutable active-point set.
   207	            // Dynamic scheduling balances seeds whose clusters grow to
   208	            // different sizes.
   209	#pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
   210	            for (int position = 0;
   211	                 position < static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
   212	                 ++position) {
   213	                const int seed = unclustered_indices[position];
   214	                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
   215	                    position, unclustered_indices, distances, threshold, N,
   216	                    workspace);
   217	
   218	                if (cardinality > local_best.cardinality ||
   219	                    (cardinality == local_best.cardinality &&
   220	                     seed < local_best.seed_point)) {
   221	                    local_best.cardinality = cardinality;
   222	                    local_best.seed_point = seed;
   223	                    local_best.members = workspace.members;
   224	                }
   225	            }
   226	
   227	            // The serial choice preserves the original deterministic rule:
   228	            // largest cardinality, then the first (lowest-index) seed.
   229	#pragma omp single
   230	            {
   231	                const int team_size = omp_get_num_threads();
   232	                int winner = -1;
   233	                for (int thread = 0; thread < team_size; ++thread) {
   234	                    if (thread_bests[thread].cardinality < 0) {
   235	                        continue;
   236	                    }
   237	                    if (winner < 0 ||
   238	                        thread_bests[thread].cardinality >
   239	                            thread_bests[winner].cardinality ||
   240	                        (thread_bests[thread].cardinality ==
   241	                             thread_bests[winner].cardinality &&
   242	                         thread_bests[thread].seed_point <
   243	                             thread_bests[winner].seed_point)) {
   244	                        winner = thread;
   245	                    }
   246	                }
   247	
   248	                if (winner >= 0 && thread_bests[winner].cardinality > 0) {
   249	                    Cluster cluster;
   250	                    cluster.seed_point = thread_bests[winner].seed_point;
   251	                    cluster.members = thread_bests[winner].members;
   252	
   253	                    for (const int member : cluster.members) {
   254	                        selected[member] = 1;
   255	                    }
   256	
   257	                    unclustered_indices.erase(
   258	                        std::remove_if(
   259	                            unclustered_indices.begin(),
   260	                            unclustered_indices.end(),
   261	                            [&selected](const int point) {
   262	                                return selected[point] != 0;
   263	                            }),
   264	                        unclustered_indices.end());
   265	                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
   266	                } else {
   267	                    done = true;
   268	                }
   269	
   270	                if (unclustered_indices.empty()) {
   271	                    done = true;
   272	                }
   273	            }
   274	            // The implicit single barrier publishes the updated active set and
   275	            // done flag before every thread evaluates the next iteration.
   276	        }
   277	    }
   278	
   279	    return clusters;
   280	}
   281	
   282	// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties
   283	bool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,
   284	                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
   285	                     const double threshold) {
   286	    bool valid = true;
   287	    
   288	    printf("Validating clusters:\n");
   289	    
   290	    // Check each cluster
   291	    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
   292	        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];
   293	        double max_diameter = 0.0;
   294	        
   295	        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)
   296	        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {
   297	            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {
   298	                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], 
   299	                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);
   300	                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);
   301	            }
   302	        }
   303	        
   304	        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters
   305	            printf("  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\n", 
   306	                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);
   307	        }
   308	        
   309	        // Validate diameter is within threshold
   310	        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error
   311	            printf("ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\n", 
   312	                   c, max_diameter, threshold);
   313	            valid = false;
   314	        }
   315	    }
   316	    
   317	    // Check for duplicate memberships
   318	    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);
   319	    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
   320	        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
   321	            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];
   322	            if (membership[member] >= 0) {
   323	                printf("ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\n",
   324	                       member, membership[member], c);
   325	                valid = false;
   326	            }
   327	            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);
   328	        }
   329	    }
   330

 // Main QT clustering algorithm
 std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                   const double threshold) {
     const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
-    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
     std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;

     }

-    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {

-                                                       threshold, N, 
-                                                       &candidate_members);

-            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
-                max_cardinality = cardinality;
-                best_seed = seed;
-                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
+
+    // Distances are reused by many seed candidates.  A full row-major matrix
+    // makes the update for a newly-added member a cache-friendly row traversal
+    // and computes each directed distance only once.
+    const size_t matrix_size = static_cast<size_t>(N) * static_cast<size_t>(N);
+    std::vector<double> distances(matrix_size);
+
+    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();
+    std::vector<CandidateWorkspace> workspaces(max_threads);
+    std::vector<ThreadBest> thread_bests(max_threads);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> selected(N, 0);
+    bool done = unclustered_indices.empty();
+
+    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all QT iterations.  This is important
+    // as clusters get smaller: repeatedly creating a team would eventually
+    // cost more than the remaining candidate work.
+#pragma omp parallel shared(done, distances, unclustered_indices, clusters, workspaces, thread_bests, selected)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        CandidateWorkspace& workspace = workspaces[thread_id];
+        ThreadBest& local_best = thread_bests[thread_id];
+
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (int member = 0; member < N; ++member) {
+            double* distance_row =
+                distances.data() + static_cast<size_t>(member) * N;
+#pragma omp simd
+            for (int candidate = 0; candidate < N; ++candidate) {
+                // Keep the argument order identical to the sequential search.
+                distance_row[candidate] =
+                    distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
             }
         }
-

-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+        while (!done) {
+            local_best.reset();
+
+            // Every seed candidate reads the same immutable active-point set.
+            // Dynamic scheduling balances seeds whose clusters grow to
+            // different sizes.
+#pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (int position = 0;
+                 position < static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                 ++position) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[position];
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    position, unclustered_indices, distances, threshold, N,
+                    workspace);
+
+                if (cardinality > local_best.cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best.cardinality &&
+                     seed < local_best.seed_point)) {
+                    local_best.cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best.seed_point = seed;
+                    local_best.members = workspace.members;
+                }
+            }
+
+            // The serial choice preserves the original deterministic rule:
+            // largest cardinality, then the first (lowest-index) seed.
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                const int team_size = omp_get_num_threads();
+                int winner = -1;
+                for (int thread = 0; thread < team_size; ++thread) {
+                    if (thread_bests[thread].cardinality < 0) {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    if (winner < 0 ||
+                        thread_bests[thread].cardinality >
+                            thread_bests[winner].cardinality ||
+                        (thread_bests[thread].cardinality ==
+                             thread_bests[winner].cardinality &&
+                         thread_bests[thread].seed_point <
+                             thread_bests[winner].seed_point)) {
+                        winner = thread;
+                    }
+                }
+
+                if (winner >= 0 && thread_bests[winner].cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = thread_bests[winner].seed_point;
+                    cluster.members = thread_bests[winner].members;
+
+                    for (const int member : cluster.members) {
+                        selected[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(
+                            unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                            unclustered_indices.end(),
+                            [&selected](const int point) {
+                                return selected[point] != 0;
+                            }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+                } else {
+                    done = true;
+                }
+
+                if (unclustered_indices.empty()) {
+                    done = true;
+                }
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+            // The implicit single barrier publishes the updated active set and
+            // done flag before every thread evaluates the next iteration.
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

+// Per-thread storage is retained for the whole clustering operation.  This
+// avoids allocating O(N) temporary arrays for every seed point.
+struct alignas(64) CandidateWorkspace {
+    std::vector<double> max_distances;
+    std::vector<int> members;
+};
+
+struct alignas(64) ThreadBest {
+    int cardinality = -1;
+    int seed_point = -1;
+    std::vector<int> members;
+
+    void reset() {
+        cardinality = -1;
+        seed_point = -1;
+        members.clear();
+    }

 // Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point

+                              const std::vector<int>& unclustered_indices,

+    const int active_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());

+    workspace.members.clear();

+        // original lowest-index tie breaking.

 // Main QT clustering algorithm
 std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                   const double threshold) {
     const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
-    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
     std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
     std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
-    
+
     // Initialize unclustered indices
+    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);
+    clusters.reserve(N);
     for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
         unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
     }
-    
-    // Main clustering loop
-    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
-        int max_cardinality = -1;
-        int best_seed = -1;
-        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
-        
-        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
-            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
-            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
-            
-            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
-            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
-                                                       threshold, N, 
-                                                       &candidate_members);
-            
-            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
-                max_cardinality = cardinality;
-                best_seed = seed;
-                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
+
+    // Distances are reused by many seed candidates.  A full row-major matrix
+    // makes the update for a newly-added member a cache-friendly row traversal
+    // and computes each directed distance only once.
+    const size_t matrix_size = static_cast<size_t>(N) * static_cast<size_t>(N);
+    std::vector<double> distances(matrix_size);
+
+    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();
+    std::vector<CandidateWorkspace> workspaces(max_threads);
+    std::vector<ThreadBest> thread_bests(max_threads);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> selected(N, 0);
+    bool done = unclustered_indices.empty();
+
+    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all QT iterations.  This is important
+    // as clusters get smaller: repeatedly creating a team would eventually
+    // cost more than the remaining candidate work.
+#pragma omp parallel shared(done, distances, unclustered_indices, clusters, workspaces, thread_bests, selected)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        CandidateWorkspace& workspace = workspaces[thread_id];
+        ThreadBest& local_best = thread_bests[thread_id];
+
+#pragma omp for schedule(static)
+        for (int member = 0; member < N; ++member) {
+            double* distance_row =
+                distances.data() + static_cast<size_t>(member) * N;
+#pragma omp simd
+            for (int candidate = 0; candidate < N; ++candidate) {
+                // Keep the argument order identical to the sequential search.
+                distance_row[candidate] =
+                    distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
             }
         }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+        while (!done) {
+            local_best.reset();
+
+            // Every seed candidate reads the same immutable active-point set.
+            // Dynamic scheduling balances seeds whose clusters grow to
+            // different sizes.
+#pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (int position = 0;
+                 position < static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                 ++position) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[position];
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    position, unclustered_indices, distances, threshold, N,
+                    workspace);
+
+                if (cardinality > local_best.cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best.cardinality &&
+                     seed < local_best.seed_point)) {
+                    local_best.cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best.seed_point = seed;
+                    local_best.members = workspace.members;
+                }
+            }
+
+            // The serial choice preserves the original deterministic rule:
+            // largest cardinality, then the first (lowest-index) seed.
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                const int team_size = omp_get_num_threads();
+                int winner = -1;
+                for (int thread = 0; thread < team_size; ++thread) {
+                    if (thread_bests[thread].cardinality < 0) {
+                        continue;
+                    }
+                    if (winner < 0 ||
+                        thread_bests[thread].cardinality >
+                            thread_bests[winner].cardinality ||
+                        (thread_bests[thread].cardinality ==
+                             thread_bests[winner].cardinality &&
+                         thread_bests[thread].seed_point <
+                             thread_bests[winner].seed_point)) {
+                        winner = thread;
+                    }
+                }
+
+                if (winner >= 0 && thread_bests[winner].cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = thread_bests[winner].seed_point;
+                    cluster.members = thread_bests[winner].members;
+
+                    for (const int member : cluster.members) {
+                        selected[member] = 1;
+                    }
+
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(
+                            unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                            unclustered_indices.end(),
+                            [&selected](const int point) {
+                                return selected[point] != 0;
+                            }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+                } else {
+                    done = true;
+                }
+
+                if (unclustered_indices.empty()) {
+                    done = true;
+                }
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
+            // The implicit single barrier publishes the updated active set and
+            // done flag before every thread evaluates the next iteration.
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }
